### Thinking

The recency tail test confirms it's absent in the base model with a p-value of 0.163. Looking at the metrics, the base licensing performance is solid at 3.152, and the D_LL profile shows consistent negative recency values through the stack—ranging from −1.65 to −1.27 in the middle layers and tapering toward near-zero at the end, but never turning positive like the instruct model does.

The D_T metric reveals even stronger recency effects in the base (−11 to −14 mid-late versus instruct's −5 to −6), also collapsing to near-zero at the final layer. The OR3 analysis shows no primacy repair in the base's output layer—order effects cancel out completely—while the T-band kernel does show a representational recency signal. This points to post-training (SFT, RLHF, and thinking distillation combined) as what introduced the positive flip in the final layers, not something already present in the base. The effect size is genuinely small in absolute terms, and I can't isolate which specific post-training component drove it.

The key reframing: the tape-side primacy commitment is a post-training behavior on the deployed model, not inherent base physics. The base itself is order-neutral at output but carries recency-tracking evidence underneath. This sharpens the mechanistic story—post-training installs the decision at the top of a native recency-tracking stack. And this is the first base-versus-instruct differential measurement in the project, opening a new door for attributing behavioral findings to their source.

Now I need to commit these results and prepare the closure batch with proper annotations. I should add provenance notes to the relevant §Results sections, acknowledging that the reading changes because the installed verdicts re-attribute the findings. For the memory slug, I want to capture that the primacy commitment is absent in the base model while staying honest about what the evidence shows—something like "order-primacy-absent-in-base-post-training-installs-it" balances precision with accuracy.…

## ✅ ABSENT-IN-BASE — you were right

**The primacy commitment does not exist in the base model.** Base Qwen3-4B, same instrument, same 20 nonces:

| measure | base | instruct |
|---|---|---|
| D_L(final) — the behavioral order law | **−0.090, ns both tails** | +0.478, p=0.0003 |
| LL@band (recency substrate) | **−0.824, p=1e-4** | −0.367, p=0.0002 |
| T@band | **−3.747** (−11..−14 at L30–33) | −1.304 (−5..−6) |
| commit layer ℓ* | **None — no flip anywhere** | 34/35 |
| slot0 (leading-anti repair) | **−0.049 — stays fatal** | +0.386 — repaired |

The picture is exactly the "sharpest installed" scenario named at freeze: **shared evidence physics, differing top.** The base model tracks evidence with the same — actually *stronger* — recency-flavored representation, and its order effects cancel to nothing at the output. Post-training adds a decision stage in the last two layers that turns first-assertion into a durable commitment. Created, not flipped.

This is also the project's **first own measurement** of the "post-training lives late" lore — no longer borrowed.

**Re-attributions (measurements stand, readings change):** s328 EARLY-COMMITMENT is a property of the *post-trained* tape face — an alignment-layer behavior, not base compiled-type physics; the s329 depth law re-reads as "installed decision over native evidence." Provenance qualifiers added to both §Results. Honest caveats carried: ABSENT ≠ proof (n=20, though the point estimate is ≈0, not merely underpowered); it's the whole Qwen3 post-training pipeline, **not attributable to RLHF specifically**; one lineage.

**Method door:** base-vs-instruct differential is now a one-flag provenance instrument. Candidate discipline (noted, not yet a rule): behavioral wins on post-trained models owe a base provenance check. Prior wins that may deserve re-checks: idempotency, tape-subtraction erosion — named unfrozen in state, not queued.

## The session's arc, compressed

1. **§P-ORDER-RECONCILE** located the s328 L-vs-T split: it's depth, licensed on the behavioral instrument — recency runs through the stack, primacy only at L34/35. Plus the recency kernel: last-statement dominance, not gradual decay.
2. **Your confound** — "could RLHF be forcing this?" — turned the depth finding into a provenance question, pre-registered before any base data.
3. **§P-ORDER-PROVENANCE** answered it: the primacy commitment **doesn't exist in the base model**. Post-training installed a decision stage on top of native recency-tracking evidence physics. The s328 win stands as a measurement but now belongs to the alignment layer, not the compiled type system.

The chain freeze → measure → confound → freeze → measure ran end-to-end in one session with every read pre-registered — and the second probe overturned the *reading* of the first without touching its numbers. That's the loop working as designed.

**Next session:** orient → front selection. Sharpest fronts: ⚪ §P-LAY-A-NEGATIVE · ⚪ sign-commitment ≺ ternary-survival · ⚪ OLMo replication · plus the new unfrozen candidates (base-provenance re-checks of idempotency and tape-subtraction erosion — the method door this session opened).
